  1   | import { test as base, expect, type Locator, type Page } from "@playwright/test";
  2   | import { allLessons } from "../../src/data/curriculum";
  3   | import { mockQuestions } from "../../src/data/mock";
  4   | import type { ProgressSnapshot, Question } from "../../src/data/types";
  5   | import { answerTalk } from "./live";
  6   | import { type ProgressSeed, seedWrites } from "./progressSeed";
  7   | 
  8   | export const test = base.extend<{ blockExternal: void }>({
  9   |   blockExternal: [
  10  |     async ({ context }, provide) => {
  11  |       await context.route("**/*", async (route) => {
  12  |         const url = new URL(route.request().url());

  113 | 
  114 | // A tile can hold several words ("tu amigo?"), so the answer is split into the tiles that build it.
  115 | const tilesInAnswerOrder = (q: Question) => {
  116 |   const tiles: string[] = [];
  117 |   const left = [...(q.tokens ?? [])];
  118 |   let rest = q.answer;
  119 | 
  120 |   while (rest) {
  121 |     const tile = left.filter((t) => rest.startsWith(t)).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length)[0];
  122 | 
  123 |     if (!tile) {
  124 |       return q.answer.split(" ");
  125 |     }
  126 | 
  127 |     tiles.push(tile);
  128 |     left.splice(left.indexOf(tile), 1);
  129 |     rest = rest.slice(tile.length).trimStart();
  130 |   }
  131 | 
  132 |   return tiles;
  133 | };
  134 |

  139 |         name: wrong ? q.options.find((o) => o !== q.answer)! : q.answer,
  140 |         exact: true,
  141 |       })
  142 |       .click();
  143 | 
  144 |     return;
  145 |   } else if (q.kind === "order") {
  146 |     for (const tile of tilesInAnswerOrder(q)) {

  148 |         .locator(".word-bank")
  149 |         .getByRole("button", { name: tile, exact: true, disabled: false })
  150 |         .filter({ visible: true })
  151 |         .first()
  152 |         .click();
  153 |     }
  154 | 
  155 |     return;
